<!-- AndroidManifest.xml -->
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools">

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- ÐÀÇÐÅØÅÍÈß                                                   -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->

    <!-- Äëÿ ïîëó÷åíèÿ èíôîðìàöèè î òåëåôîíå (óðîâåíü ñèãíàëà, òèï ñåòè) -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.READ_PHONE_STATE" />

    <!-- Äëÿ ïðîâåðêè ñîñòîÿíèÿ ñåòè -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />

    <!-- Äëÿ ïðîâåðêè ñîñòîÿíèÿ Wi-Fi -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_WIFI_STATE" />

    <!-- Äëÿ äîñòóïà â èíòåðíåò (ïðîâåðêà îáíîâëåíèé, çàãðóçêà îáîëî÷åê) -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />

    <!-- Äëÿ îïðåäåëåíèÿ ìåñòîïîëîæåíèÿ (ñòàòóñ GPS) -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION" />

    <!-- ÄËß ÓÑÒÀÍÎÂÊÈ APK (Android 8+) -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES"
        tools:ignore="RequestInstallPackagesPolicy" />

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- QUERIES                                                      -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->

    <queries>
        <!-- Ïðèëîæåíèÿ ñ èêîíêîé íà ðàáî÷åì ñòîëå (äëÿ GetApps) -->
        <intent>
            <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
            <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
        </intent>
        <!-- Home-ïðèëîæåíèÿ (äëÿ ïðîâåðêè òåêóùåãî ëàóí÷åðà ïî óìîë÷àíèþ) -->
        <intent>
            <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
            <category android:name="android.intent.category.HOME" />
        </intent>
    </queries>

    <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- ÏÐÈËÎÆÅÍÈÅ                                                   -->
    <!-- ============================================================ -->

    <application
        android:allowBackup="true"
        android:dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"
        android:fullBackupContent="@xml/backup_rules"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:roundIcon="@mipmap/ic_launcher_round"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.QutyLaunch"
        android:requestLegacyExternalStorage="false"
        tools:ignore="UnusedAttribute">

        <!-- ============================================================ -->
        <!-- ÀÊÒÈÂÍÎÑÒÈ                                                   -->
        <!-- ============================================================ -->

        <!-- Ãëàâíûé ýêðàí (WebView + HOME-ëàóí÷åð) -->
        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:launchMode="singleTask"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.HOME" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.DEFAULT" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <!-- Ýêðàí ïðèâåòñòâèÿ (îíáîðäèíã) -->
        <activity
            android:name=".WelcomeActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.QutyLaunch"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden">
        </activity>

        <!-- Ëîããåð -->
        <activity
            android:name=".LoggerActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.AppCompat.Translucent">
        </activity>

        <!-- Íàñòðîéêè -->
        <activity
            android:name=".SettingsActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.AppCompat.Translucent">
        </activity>

        <!-- Ìàãàçèí -->
        <activity
            android:name=".StoreActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.AppCompat.Translucent">
        </activity>

        <!-- Òåðìèíàë -->
        <activity
            android:name=".TerminalActivity"
            android:exported="false"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.AppCompat.Translucent">
        </activity>

        <!-- ============================================================ -->
        <!-- FILE PROVIDER                                                -->
        <!-- ============================================================ -->

        <provider
            android:name="androidx.core.content.FileProvider"
            android:authorities="${applicationId}.fileprovider"
            android:exported="false"
            android:grantUriPermissions="true">

            <meta-data
                android:name="android.support.FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS"
                android:resource="@xml/file_paths" />

        </provider>

    </application>

</manifest>

<LinearLayout xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:app="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res-auto"
    android:layout_width="match_parent"
    android:layout_height="match_parent"
    android:background="?android:attr/colorBackground"
    android:baselineAligned="false"
    android:orientation="horizontal">

    <!-- ËÅÂÀß ÏÀÍÅËÜ (ÌÅÍÞ) -->
    <LinearLayout
        android:id="@+id/menu_pane"
        android:layout_width="340dp"
        android:layout_height="match_parent"
        android:background="?attr/colorSurface"
        android:orientation="vertical">

        <!-- ØÀÏÊÀ ÌÅÍÞ Ñ ÃÐÀÄÈÅÍÒÎÌ -->
        <LinearLayout
            android:layout_width="match_parent"
            android:layout_height="wrap_content"
            android:orientation="horizontal"
            android:gravity="center_vertical"
            android:paddingStart="@dimen/spacing_m"
            android:paddingEnd="@dimen/spacing_m"
            android:paddingTop="@dimen/spacing_m"
            android:paddingBottom="@dimen/spacing_xs"
            android:background="@drawable/bg_toolbar_gradient"
            android:clipChildren="false"
            android:clipToPadding="false"
            android:elevation="4dp">

            <TextView
                style="@style/Text.Title.Large"
                android:layout_width="0dp"
                android:layout_height="wrap_content"
                android:layout_weight="1"
                android:padding="@dimen/spacing_sm"
                android:text="@string/logger_title" />

            <LinearLayout
                android:id="@+id/btn_close"
                android:layout_width="wrap_content"
                android:layout_height="wrap_content"
                android:layout_marginStart="@dimen/spacing_m"

                android:padding="@dimen/spacing_s"
                android:clickable="true"
                android:focusable="true">

                <ImageButton
                    android:layout_width="@dimen/icon_medium"
                    android:layout_height="@dimen/icon_medium"
                    android:background="@android:color/transparent"
                    android:contentDescription="@string/settings_close"
                    android:src="@drawable/ic_close"
                    app:tint="?attr/iconTintColor"
                    android:clickable="false"
                    android:focusable="false" />

            </LinearLayout>

        </LinearLayout>

        <androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
            android:id="@+id/recycler_menu"
            android:layout_width="match_parent"
            android:layout_height="0dp"
            android:layout_weight="1"
            android:clipToPadding="false"
            android:padding="@dimen/spacing_s"
            android:scrollbars="vertical" />

    </LinearLayout>

    <!-- ÏÐÀÂÀß ÏÀÍÅËÜ (ÊÎÍÒÅÍÒ) -->

        android:id="@+id/content_pane"
        android:layout_width="0dp"

        android:clipChildren="false"
        android:clipToPadding="false">

        <androidx.constraintlayout.widget.ConstraintLayout
            android:layout_width="match_parent"
            android:layout_height="match_parent"
            android:background="@android:color/transparent"
            android:clipChildren="false"
            android:clipToPadding="false">

            <!-- ÔÐÀÃÌÅÍÒ ÊÎÍÒÅÍÒÀ -->

                android:id="@+id/fragment_container"

                app:layout_constraintStart_toStartOf="parent"

                <!-- Êíîïêà ÍÀÇÀÄ / ÌÅÍÞ -->
